Hooker]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 248|UFC 248: Adesanya vs. Romero]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 169''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 27''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 29, 2020 at [[Chartway Arena]] in [[Norfolk, Virginia]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/12/ufc-return-norfolk-virginia-february-mma|title=UFC schedules return to Virginia for Feb. 29 in Norfolk|date=2019-12-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|access-date=2019-12-14}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/12/13/21020767/ufc-returns-to-norfolk-va-on-feb-29|title=UFC returns to Norfolk, Va. on Feb. 29|author=Newswire|date=2019-12-13|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2019-12-14}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has hosted in Norfolk and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Poirier vs. Pettis]] in November 2017.\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former title challenger [[Joseph Benavidez]] and [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] served as the event headliner. Multi-divisional champion [[Henry Cejudo]] announced on December 19, 2019 that he would be relinquishing the flyweight title and concentrate on competing solely in the bantamweight division.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/12/henry-cejudo-stripped-flyweight-title-joseph-benavidez-deivseon-figuerido-targeted-ufc-norfolk|title=Henry Cejudo relinquishing UFC flyweight belt; Joseph Benavidez-Deiveson Figueiredo targeted|date=2019-12-19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|access-date=2019-12-20}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Alex Oliveira (fighter)|Alex Oliveira]] and [[Mickey Gall]] was scheduled for the event. However, the bout was pulled from the event on December 27 due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/12/27/21040025/mickey-gall-vs-alex-oliveira-pulled-from-ufc-norfolk|title=Mickey Gall vs. Alex Oliveira pulled from UFC Norfolk|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2019-12-27|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2019-12-28}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Chas Skelly]] and [[Grant Dawson]] was scheduled to take place at [[UFC 246]], but Dawson was forced to withdraw from the bout.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/ufc-246-grant-dawson-vs-chas-skelly-canceled|title=Grant Dawson says UFC 246 fight vs. Chas Skelly has been canceled|author=Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2020-01-14|access-date=2020-01-14}} The pairing was then rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/chas-skelly-vs-grant-dawson-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-voor-ufc-norfolk/|title=Chas Skelly vs. Grant Dawson rescheduled, this time for UFC Norfolk|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Redactie|date=2020-01-23|access-date=2020-01-23}}{{in lang|nl}} In turn, it was reported on February 7 that Skelly was injured during one of his training sessions and therefore forced to withdraw from the fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/6/21126742/chas-skelly-announces-injury-withdrawal-from-ufc-norfolk|title=Chas Skelly announces injury withdrawal from UFC Norfolk|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-02-06|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2020-02-07}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Darrick Minner]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/ufc-norfolk-darrick-minner-short-notice-replacement-vs-grant-dawson|title=34-fight veteran Darrick Minner to fight Grant Dawson at UFC on ESPN+ 27 on short notice|date=2020-02-20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|access-date=2020-02-21}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Steven Peterson]] and Aalon Cruz was scheduled to take place at the event. However, Peterson was pulled from the event for an unknown reason and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Spike Carlyle]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/spike-carlyle-maakt-debuut-op-short-notice-tegen-aalon-cruz-tijdens-ufc-norfolk/|title=Spike Carlyle makes debut on short notice against Aalon Cruz during UFC Norfolk|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Redactie|date=2020-02-07|access-date=2020-02-07}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA featherweight bout between Mike Davis and Giga Chikadze expected to take place at this event was cancelled after Davis pulled out due to an undisclosed injury. In turn, promotion officials elected to remove Chikadze from the card due to being unable to find a replacement and scheduled him to face Jamall Emmers at [[UFC 248]], replacing an injured Movsar Evloev.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/giga-chikadze-vervangt-movsar-evloev-tegen-jamall-emmers-tijdens-ufc-248-in-las-vegas/|title=Giga Chikadze replaces Movsar Evloev against Jamall Emmers during UFC 248 in Las Vegas|author=Redactie|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-02-27|access-date=2020-02-27}}{{in lang|nl}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Luis Peña (fighter)|Luis Peña]] and Alexander Muñoz was scheduled to take place at the event. However, it was announced on February 23 that Muñoz had pulled out due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/ufc-norfolk-alex-munoz-injury-withdrawal-vs-luis-pena|title=Alex Munoz forced out at UFC Norfolk, Luis Pena needs new opponent|date=2020-02-22|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan |access-date=2020-02-23}} He was replaced by Steve Garcia.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/steve-garcia-replaces-alex-munoz-takes-on-luis-pena-at-ufc-on-espn-27|title=Steve Garcia replaces Alex Munoz, takes on Luis Pena at UFC on ESPN+ 27|date=2020-02-24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|access-date=2020-02-24}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Figueiredo came in at 127.5 pounds, 2.5 pounds over the flyweight title fight limit of 125 pounds. As a result, he was ineligible to win the championship, while Benavidez remained eligible if he won.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/deiveson-figueiredo-misses-weight-ufc-on-espn-plus-27-norfolk-title-fight-benavidez-video|title=Deiveson Figueiredo misses weight; only Joseph Benavidez eligible for title at UFC on ESPN+ 27|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-02-28|access-date=2020-02-28}} Figueiredo was fined 30% of his purse, which went to Benavidez and the bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight. Dawson also failed to make weight, coming in at 149.5 pounds, 3.5 pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit of 146 pounds. He was also fined 30% of his fight purse, which went to Minner and the bout proceeded at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/ufc-on-espn-plus-27-norfolk-joseph-benavidez-deiveson-figueiredo-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN+ 27 weigh-in results: Two fighters overweight, including a headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-02-28|access-date=2020-02-28}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (127.5 lb)\n|[[Deiveson Figueiredo]]\n|def.\n|[[Joseph Benavidez]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|1:54\n|For the vacant [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] (only Benavidez was eligible).\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Felicia Spencer]]\n|def.\n|[[Zarah Fairn]]\n|TKO (elbows and punches)\n|1\n|3:37\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Magomed Ankalaev]]\n|def.\n|[[Ion Cuțelaba]]\n|TKO (head kicks and punches)\n|1\n|0:38\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Megan Anderson (fighter)|Megan Anderson]]\n|def.\n|[[Norma Dumont]]\n|KO (punch)\n|1\n|3:31\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (149.5 lb)\n|[[Grant Dawson]]\n|def. \n|[[Darrick Minner]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|2\n|1:38\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Kyler Phillips]]\n|def.\n|Gabriel Silva\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Brendan Allen]]\n|def.\n|[[Tom Breese]]\n|TKO (elbows and punches)\n|1\n|4:47\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Marcin Tybura]]\n|def.\n|[[Serghei Spivac]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Luis Peña (fighter)|Luis Peña]]\n|def.\n|[[Steve Garcia]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Jordan Griffin]]\n|def.\n|[[T.J. It does not include sponsor money or \"locker room\" bonuses often given by the UFC and also do not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. Hannoun|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2019-12-03}}\n\n==Background==\nA lightweight bout between former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Kevin Lee (fighter)|Kevin Lee]] and [[Charles Oliveira]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/1/14/21065576/kevin-lee-charles-oliveira-ufc-brasilia-mma|title=Kevin Lee vs. Charles Oliveira set to headline UFC Brasilia in March|date=2020-01-14|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|access-date=2020-01-14}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Brad Tavares]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] heavyweight winner [[Antônio Carlos Júnior]] was scheduled for the event. However, Tavares was forced to pull out of the fight due to an [[anterior cruciate ligament]] (ACL) injury.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/brad-tavares-sofre-lesao-e-esta-fora-da-luta-contra-cara-de-sapato-no-ufc-brasilia.ghtml|title=Brad Tavares injured and out of fight against Cara de Sapato at UFC Brasília|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone and Raphael Marinho|date=2019-12-31|access-date=2019-12-31}} {{in lang|pt}} He was replaced by [[Makhmud Muradov]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Makhmud-Muradov-Steps-in-Against-Antonio-Carlos-Jr-at-UFC-Brasilia-168293|title=Makhmud Muradov steps in against Antonio Carlos Jr. at UFC Brasilia|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2020-01-04|access-date=2020-01-04}} In turn, Júnior suffered an unspecified injury and the bout was cancelled from the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/antonio-carlos-junior-injured-makhmud-muradov-fight-scratched-from-ufc-on-espn-28|title=Antonio Carlos Junior injured, Makhmud Muradov fight scratched from UFC on ESPN+ 28|date=2020-01-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|access-date=2020-01-14}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Paige VanZant]] and [[Amanda Ribas]] was scheduled for the event. However, VanZant was forced to pull out of the fight due to an undisclosed injury. VanZant requested to reschedule the bout one month later, but Ribas decided to remain at the event and [[Randa Markos]] stepped in as the replacement.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Paige-VanZant-Out-Randa-Markos-In-Against-Amanda-Ribas-at-UFC-Brasilia-169165|title=Report: Paige VanZant out, Randa Markos in against Amanda Ribas at UFC Brasilia|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2020-01-24|access-date=2020-01-24}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Su Mudaerji]] and [[Bruno Gustavo da Silva]] was scheduled for the event. However, due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic in mainland China]], Mudaerji was forced to withdraw from the event as he would not travel to prepare for the fight and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[David Dvořák]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/epidemia-de-coronavirus-complica-treinos-de-su-mudaerji-no-tibete-e-atleta-sai-do-card-do-ufc-brasilia.ghtml|title=Coronavirus epidemic complicates Su Mudaerji's training in Tibet and athlete leaves UFC card|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone|date=2020-02-08|access-date=2020-02-08}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Lee weighed in at 158.5 lb, 2.5 lb over the lightweight non-title fight limit of 156 pounds. He was fined 20% of his purse and his bout with Oliveira proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-on-espn-plus-28-brasilia-kevin-lee-charles-oliveira-weigh-in-results/amp?__twitter_impression=true|title=UFC on ESPN+ 28 weigh-in results: Kevin Lee misses weight|date=2020-03-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|access-date=2020-03-13|archive-date=2020-06-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200604003458/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-on-espn-plus-28-brasilia-kevin-lee-charles-oliveira-weigh-in-results/amp?__twitter_impression=true|url-status=dead}}\n\n===COVID-19 pandemic===\nOn March 12, the UFC announced that this event would take place [[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]] (for the first time in the promotion's history) after Ibaneis Rocha, the [[Governor#Brazil|governor]] of the [[Federal District (Brazil)|Federal District]] (where Brasília is located), announced that large gatherings will be suspended or closed for a period of 5 days due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]]. Both the UFC's planned Thursday media day and Friday ceremonial weigh-ins were cancelled ahead of the card, with the official early morning weigh-ins being the only official fighter obligations that remained ahead of fight night. In response, UFC president [[Dana White]] stated \"We [The UFC] always go overboard with health and safety, and that’s what we’re going to do here\", although none of the fighters were [[COVID-19 testing|tested for the COVID-19 virus]] by the commission leading up to the event.{{Cite web|url=https://au.sports.yahoo.com/dana-white-ufc-coronavirus-president-donald-trump-022940222.html|title=After urging from President Trump, Dana White says he'll keep holding UFC events despite coronavirus|publisher=au.sports.yahoo.com|author=Jack Baer|date=2020-03-13|access-date=2020-03-13}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/3/13/21179202/ufc-brasilia-fighters-are-not-being-tested-by-commission-for-coronavirus-lee-oliveira-mma-espn|title=UFC Brasilia fighters not being tested for Coronavirus|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2020-03-13|publisher=mmamania.com|access-date=2020-03-13}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent fight|date=2019-12-02|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|access-date=2019-12-14}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Israel Adesanya]] and former interim title challenger (as well as [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics|2000 Olympic silver medalist]] and former world champion in [[freestyle wrestling]]) [[Yoel Romero]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/israel-adesanya-vs-yoel-romero-ufc-248-title-fight-booked|title=Israel Adesanya defends middleweight title against Yoel Romero at UFC 248|date=2020-01-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Nolan King|access-date=2020-01-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Zhang Weili]] and former champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] took place as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/strawweight-champ-weili-zhang-to-fight-at-ufc-248-in-las-vegas-1913787/|title=Strawweight champ Weili Zhang to fight at UFC 248 in Las Vegas|date=2019-12-13|publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Adam Hill|access-date=2019-12-14}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former champion [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) and [[Jared Cannonier]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.dailytelegraph.com.au/sport/boxing-mma/robert-whittaker-to-make-his-ufc-return-against-jared-cannonier-on-march-7/news-story/71ad8f50d72e647362f7f6eece5b805a|title=Whittaker's next UFC bout confirmed|date=2019-12-20|publisher=dailytelegraph.com.au|author=Walshaw|access-date=2020-01-15|first=Nick}} However, it was announced on January 15 that Whittaker pulled out of the bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/ufc-248-robert-whittaker-out-jared-cannonieir-fight-report|title=Robert Whittaker out of UFC 248 fight against Jared Cannonier|date=2020-01-15|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=King|access-date=2020-03-02|first=Nolan}} Subsequently, promotion officials announced that Cannonier would be on standby in the event that one of the main event participants were to pull out of that contest.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/2/9/21130999/ufc-taps-jared-cannonier-as-backup-fighter-for-ufc-248-adesanya-vs-romero-main-event|title=UFC taps Jared Cannonier as backup fighter for UFC 248 Adesanya vs. Romero main event|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2020-02-08|publisher=mmamania.com|access-date=2020-02-10}} In turn, Cannonier indicated in mid-February that he had suffered a torn pectoral injury while training and would be sidelined for several months.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Middleweight-Contender-Jared-Cannonier-Targets-Fall-Return-After-Suffering-Torn-Pectoral-170167|title=Middleweight contender Jared Cannonier targets Fall return after suffering torn pectoral injury|author=Tristen Critchfield |date=2020-02-15|publisher=sherdog.com|access-date=2020-02-17}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] bantamweight winner [[José Alberto Quiñónez]] was originally scheduled to take place at [[UFC 229]]. However, it was scrapped after O'Malley failed a [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) drug test.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/9/30/17921592/sean-omalley-announces-hes-failed-usada-drug-test-out-of-ufc-229|title=Sean O'Malley announces he's failed USADA drug test, out of UFC 229|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Raimondi|date=2018-09-30|accessdate=2020-01-05|first=Marc}} The bout was then expected to take place at [[UFC 247]], but it was eventually moved to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/sean-omalley-jose-quinonez-moved-to-ufc-248-las-vegas|title=Sean O'Malley vs. Jose Quinonez moved to UFC 248 in Las Vegas|author=Farah Hannoun and John Morgan|date=2020-01-03|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-01-05}}\n\nWhile not officially announced by the organization, a featherweight bout between [[Calvin Kattar]] and [[Jeremy Stephens]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/calvin-kattar-vs-jeremy-stephens-featherweight-ufc-248-las-vegas|title=Calvin Kattar, Jeremy Stephens agree to UFC 248 clash in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Nolan King|access-date=2020-01-05|date=2020-01-05}} However, Stephens was removed from the card in mid-January with an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/jeremy-stephens-injured-out-ufc-248-fight-calvin-kattar|title=Jeremy Stephens injured, out of planned UFC 248 bout vs. Calvin Kattar; rescheduling expected|author= Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-01-16|date=2020-01-16}} The pairing was left intact and rescheduled for [[UFC 249]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/calvin-kattar-vs-jeremy-stephens-ufc-249|title=Calvin Kattar vs. Jeremy Stephens in the works for UFC 249|author=Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-01-23|date=2020-01-23}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Douglas Silva de Andrade]] and [[Movsar Evloev]] was scheduled to take place at the event. However, de Andrade withdrew from the bout due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jamall Emmers]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jamall-emmers-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-movsar-evloev-tijdens-ufc-248-in-las-vegas/|title=Jamall Emmers takes short notice game against Movsar Evloev during UFC 248 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Redactie|date=2020-02-12|access-date=2020-02-12}} {{in lang|nl}} Subsequently, Evloev pulled out of the fight in late February citing injuries sustained in a road accident.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/27/21156410/movsar-evloev-out-of-ufc-248-after-motorcycle-accident|title=Movsar Evloev out of UFC 248 after motorcycle accident|author=Newswire|date=2020-02-27|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2020-02-28}} He was replaced by [[Giga Chikadze]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/giga-chikadze-vervangt-movsar-evloev-tegen-jamall-emmers-tijdens-ufc-248-in-las-vegas/|title=Giga Chikadze replaces Movsar Evloev against Jamall Emmers during UFC 248 in Las Vegas|author=Redactie|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-02-27|access-date=2020-02-27}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Derek Brunson]] and [[Edmen Shahbazyan]] was scheduled to take place at the event. However, it was announced on February 20 that the bout had moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/derek-brunson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-verplaatst-van-ufc-248-naar-ufc-portland/|title=Derek Brunson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan moved from UFC 248 to UFC Portland|author=Redactie|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-02-20|access-date=2020-02-20}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Emily Whitmire]] weighed in at 117.5 pounds, 1.5 pounds over the strawweight non-title fight limit of 116 pounds. She was fined 20% of her purse and her bout with [[Polyana Viana]] was expected to proceed as scheduled at a catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-248-israel-adesanya-yoel-romero-zhang-weili-joanna-jedrzejczyk-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC 248 weigh-in results: champions, fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-248-bonuses-zhang-weili-vs-joanna-jedrzejczyk-fight-of-night|title=UFC 248 bonuses: If Zhang-Jedrzejczyk Jędrzejczyk'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Beneil Dariush''' and '''Sean O'Malley'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC). It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $2,086,000.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-248-salaries-israel-adesanya-pay-zhang-weili-total|title=UFC 248 salaries: Israel Adesanya takes home good chunk of total payout|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2020-03-09|accessdate=2020-03-09}}\n* '''Israel Adesanya:''' $500,000 (no win bonus) def. Memorial Arena]] in [[Jacksonville, Florida]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/21/21230050/ufc-249-gets-second-life-as-may-9-event-at-tba-location|title=UFC 249 gets 249 will not happen April 18|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} On April 21, the UFC confirmed that UFC 249 would be moved to May 9 and take place in [[Florida]].\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between the champion [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and interim champion [[Tony Ferguson]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner) was slated to headline this event. The pairing had previously been scheduled and cancelled for various reasons on four occasions ([[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber#The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber Finale]], [[UFC on Fox: Teixeira vs. Evans]], [[UFC 209]] and [[UFC 223]]) over the last four years.{{cite web |url= http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/30/9645620/edson-barboza-steps-in-for-khabib-nurmagomedov-to-face-tony-ace-tony-ferguson|title= Edson Barboza steps in for Khabib Nurmagomedov to face Tony Ferguson at TUF Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-10-30|access-date=2019-12-16}}{{cite web |url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-tony-ferguson-out-rashad-evans-vs-glover-teixeira-set-as-new-main-event-040516|title=Tony Ferguson out; Rashad Evans vs. Glover Teixeira set as new main event|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-05|access-date=2019-12-16}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/3/14805818/ufc-releases-statement-on-khabib-nurmagomedov|title=UFC releases statement on Khabib Nurmagomedov|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-03-03|access-date=2019-12-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2018/4/1/17187016/how-did-tony-ferguson-injure-his-knee-ufc-223|title=You won't believe how Tony Ferguson injured his knee and ended up off UFC 223|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2018-04-01|publisher=mmamania.com|access-date=2019-12-16}} The bout was cancelled for a fifth time due to issues related to the COVID-19 pandemic ''(see section below)''.\n\n[[File:Хабиб Нурмагомедов-2. 12.9.2019.jpg|thumb|left|190px|Originally, [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] was expected to make the third defense of his [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] at this event, but had to pull out due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].]]\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Jeremy Stephens]] and [[Calvin Kattar]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 248]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/1/6/21051609/ufc-248-jeremy-stephens-vs-calvin-kattar-march-7-las-vegas|title=UFC 248: Jeremy Stephens vs Calvin Kattar on tap for March 7 in Las Vegas|author=Jesse Holland|date=2020-01-06|publisher=mmamania.com|access-date=2020-02-07}} However, Stephens was removed from the card in mid-January with an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/jeremy-stephens-injured-out-ufc-248-fight-calvin-kattar|title=Jeremy Stephens injured, out of planned UFC 248 bout vs. Calvin Kattar; rescheduling expected|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|access-date=2020-01-16|date=2020-01-16}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/calvin-kattar-vs-jeremy-stephens-ufc-249|title=Calvin Kattar vs. Jeremy Stephens in the works for UFC 249|author=Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-01-23|date=2020-01-23}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and [[Ciryl Gane]] was scheduled for the event. However, it was announced on March 5 that Gane was forced to pull out of the event after he was struck by a [[pneumothorax]] in training and the bout was eventually scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21166938/ciryl-gane-out-ufc-249-fight-shamil-abdurakhimov|title=Ciryl Gane out of UFC 249 fight with Shamil Abdurakhimov|date=2020-03-05|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2020-03-06}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[List of Bellator champions#Welterweight Championship|Bellator Welterweight Champion]] [[Lyman Good]] and [[Belal Muhammad]] was scheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/ufc-249-belal-muhammad-vs-lyman-good-booked|title=Belal Muhammad vs. Lyman Good added to UFC 249 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Mike Bohn|date=2020-02-11|access-date=2020-04-04}} The pairing was previously expected to meet at [[UFC 205]] in November 2016, but Good was pulled from that event after being notified by the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) due to a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition drug test.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/lyman-good-receives-usada-notice-pulled-from-ufc-205|title=Lyman Good receives USADA notice, pulled from UFC 205|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2016-10-24|access-date=2020-04-04}} However, Good pulled out due to injury on April 4 and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/lyman-good-out-ufc-249-injury-belal-muhammad-opponent|title=Lyman Good out of UFC 249 bout with Belal Muhammad due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-04-04|access-date=2020-04-04}} It was announced on April 20 that Good actually [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]], being the first fighter to publicly acknowledge the fact.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29073417/ufc-lyman-good-now-100-testing-positive-covid-19-last-month|title=UFC's Lyman Good now '100%' after testing positive for COVID-19 last month|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}}\n\nA women's strawweight rematch between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champions]] [[Jéssica Andrade]] and [[Rose Namajunas]] was expected to co-headline the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/ufc-249-rose-namajunas-vs-jessica-andrade-rematch-booked|title=Rose Namajunas, Jessica Andrade agree to rematch at UFC 249 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-01-09|access-date=2020-04-08}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC 237]] in May 2019, where Andrade won via second round TKO to capture the title.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmaindia.com/ufc-237-results-jessica-andrade-kos-rose-namajunas-in-round-2-wins-strawweight-belt/|title=UFC 237 results – Jessica Andrade KO's Rose manager citing a pair of deaths in the family related to the COVID-19 pandemic as the reason.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/29013557/rose-namajunas-pulls-ufc-249-bout-vs-jessica-andrade|title=Rose Namajunas pulls out of UFC 249 bout vs. Jessica deaths in family|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}\n\n===COVID-19 pandemic – the April 18 date is postponed===\nOn March 12, New York [[Governor (United States)|governor]] [[Andrew Cuomo]] issued an order restricting mass gatherings and sporting events due to the COVID-19 pandemic.{{cn|date=May 2023}} Four days later, UFC president [[Dana White]] announced via a letter to employees shared to the public that this event \"is still scheduled as planned, but the location may change\".{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-postpones-three-events-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC postpones three events amid growing coronavirus outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-03-16}} On March 18, the [[New York State Athletic Commission]] (NYSAC) announced that the event would not be sanctioned to take place at the event's original site, the Barclays Center in Brooklyn.{{cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/1965609|title=Commission stops UFC 249 from happening in New York|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-03-18|access-date=2020-03-18}} On March 23, White revealed that he already had a new location for this event, but would only confirm that it will be held [[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/dana-white-says-he-has-location-for-ufc-249-confirms-closed-door-event|title=Dana White says he has location for UFC 249; confirms closed-door event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-03-23|access-date=2020-03-24}} White subsequently added on March 25 that the card could feature a different lineup, depending on the logistics.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/dana-white-ufc-249-could-add-francis-ngannou-vs-jairzinho-rozenstruik|title=Dana White: UFC 249 could add Francis Ngannou vs. Jairzinho Rozenstruik, other fights|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-03-25|access-date=2020-03-25}} \n\n[[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] stated on March 30 that he was in [[Dagestan]] and his participation at the event could be jeopardized due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28970604/khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-tony-ferguson-likely-again|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov stuck in Russia, hears new Tony Ferguson opponent sought|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-03-30|access-date=2020-03-30}} He stated on April 1 that he would probably not be competing at the event and reinforced that people should be in quarantine anyhow.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/khabib-nurmagomedov-out-of-ufc-249-fight-vs-tony-ferguson|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov confirms he's out of UFC 249: 'I can't web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/2/21205923/khabib-nurmagomedov-fires-back-at-criticism-over-not-fighting-tony-ferguson-at-ufc-249|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov fires back at criticism over not fighting Tony Ferguson at UFC 249|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-02|access-date=2020-04-04}} He later confirmed that he would compete if a location was given and they were able to move him there, but did not see it happening.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/4/21206580/khabib-nurmagomedov-ready-to-fight-but-i-dont-know-the-location-this-is-not-professional|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov ready to fight, but 'I don't know the location … this is not professional'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-04|access-date=2020-04-04}}\n\n[[File:Tachi Palace hotel.JPG|thumb|right|190px|Since [[Tachi Palace|Tachi Palace Hotel & Casino]] in [[Lemoore, California]] is located on land owned by the [[Tachi Yokuts|Tachi-Yokut Tribe]], part of the [[Santa Rosa Indian Community of the Santa Rosa Rancheria|Santa Rosa Indian Community]], the event did not fall under the jurisdiction of the [[California State Athletic Commission]] (CSAC) and their ban on combat sports events through the end of May.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/reports-ufc-249-set-to-take-place-on-california-tribal-land|title=Reports: UFC 249 finds home on California tribal land|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-10}}]]\n\nThe UFC announced on April 6 that Nurmagomedov would be replaced by former [[List of WSOF champions#Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]], with an interim title to be disputed between him and [[Tony Ferguson]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29002048/dana-white-says-tony-ferguson-justin-gaethje-headline-ufc-249|title=Dana White says Tony Ferguson-Justin Gaethje to headline UFC 249|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-04-06|access-date=2020-04-07}} The rest of the card also underwent changes, with the following fights being added as well:{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/6/21209211/ufc-announces-updated-lineup-for-ufc-249-andrade-vs-namajunas-2-still-co-headlines|title=UFC 249 fight card announced, Andrade vs. Namajunas 2 still co-headlines|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-04-06|access-date=2020-04-07}}\n\n*A heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Francis Ngannou]] and [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]]. The bout was originally scheduled to headline [[UFC on ESPN: Ngannou vs. Rozenstruik]] on March 28, but the event was cancelled due to the pandemic.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-postpones-three-events-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC postpones three events amid growing coronavirus outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-04-07}}\n*A heavyweight bout between [[Greg Hardy]] and [[Yorgan De Castro]]; the bout was originally scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Ngannou vs. Rozenstruik]].\n*A welterweight rematch between [[Vicente Luque]] and [[Niko Price]]; both fighters were expected to face different opponents at [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]]. They first met at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Machida]] in October 2017, where Luque won via second round submission.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2017/10/ufc-fight-night-119-vicente-luque-taps-niko-price-brilliant-darce-choke-sao-paulo|title=UFC Fight Night 119 results: Vicente Luque taps Niko Price with lightweight bout, but was removed from the event due to travel restrictions and replaced by [[Omar Morales (fighter)|Omar Morales]].\n*A bantamweight bout between [[Marlon Vera]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ray Borg]]; Vera was expected to compete against a different opponent at [[UFC on ESPN: Ngannou vs. Rozenstruik]].\n*[[Ottman Azaitar]] was expected to face [[Khama Worthy]] in a lightweight bout, but was removed from the event due to travel restrictions and replaced by [[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]].\n*A light heavyweight bout between [[Ryan Spann]] and [[Sam Alvey]]; Alvey was expected to compete against a different opponent at [[UFC on ESPN: Ngannou vs. Rozenstruik]].\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and [[Ion Cuțelaba]] (a rematch from their meeting at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]]) was removed.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/29/21159660/ufc-norfolk-results-magomed-ankalaev-earns-ko-over-ion-cutelaba-in-highly-controversial-stoppage|title=UFC Norfolk results: Magomed Ankalaev earns KO over Ion Cutelaba in highly controversial stoppage|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-02-29|access-date=2020-04-07}} The fight was rescheduled and took place at [[UFC 254]].\n\nThe following fights were removed from the event and were never rescheduled for future cards:\n\n*A heavyweight bout between [[Ben Rothwell]] and [[Gian Villante]].\n*A middleweight bout between [[Karl Roberson]] and [[Makhmud Muradov]].\n*A bantamweight bout between [[Hunter Azure]] and [[Umar Nurmagomedov]].\n\nA location for the event was not revealed, but Dana White said the UFC's temporary home would host U.S.-based fighters who aren't barred from traveling domestically.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/7/21211634/dana-white-covid-19-plans-weekly-fights-at-ufc-249-location-a-private-island-and-joe-rogan|title=Dana White's COVID-19 plan: Two months of weekly fights at UFC 249 location, a private island, and Joe Rogan|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-07}} On April 7, Secretary of the [[Florida Department of Business and Professional Regulation]] [[Halsey Beshears]] made a post on [[Twitter]], offering to help sanction and host UFC events in the state.{{Cite web|url=https://www.news4jax.com/news/florida/2020/04/07/regulator-opens-door-to-ufc-events-in-florida-amid-coronavirus-pandemic/|title=Regulator opens door to UFC events in Florida amid coronavirus pandemic|publisher=news4jax.com|author=Staff|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-16}} It was later revealed that the event was expected to take place at [[Tachi Palace|Tachi Palace Hotel & Casino]] in [[Lemoore, California]], the site of several events for the defunct [[World Extreme Cagefighting]] and [[Tachi Palace Fights]] promotions.{{cite web|url=https://www.nytimes.com/2020/04/07/sports/ufc-249-coronavirus-location.html|title=UFC 249, skirting coronavirus limits, is set for tribal land in California|work=[[The New York Times]]|author=Kevin Draper|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-09}}\n\nOn April 9, White announced that this event would not take place as scheduled and all other UFC events would be suspended until further notice, citing interventions from high-ranking staff of the UFC's U.S. media rightsholders, [[ESPN Inc.]] and parent [[The Walt Disney Company]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29018339/dana-white-says-ufc-249-not-happen-april-18|title=Dana White says UFC 249 will not happen April 18|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises 'Fight Island' will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} It was later revealed that the 40th governor of [[California]], [[Gavin Newsom]], influenced the decision of cancelling the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/california-gov-gavin-newsom-disney-request-to-shut-down-ufc-249|title=Report: California Gov. Gavin Newsom asked Disney to shut down UFC 249|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-10}}\n\n===May 9 – a new date is set for the event===\nThe promotion originally planned [[UFC 250]] to take place at [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo]], Brazil on May 9.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-planeja-ufc-250-em-sao-paulo-com-henry-cejudo-x-jose-aldo-de-luta-principal.ghtml|title=UFC plans UFC 250 in São Paulo with Henry Cejudo vs. José Aldo of main event|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-01-17|access-date=2020-01-17}} {{in lang|pt}} On April 7, it was announced that the venue would be used as a [[field hospital]] during the [[COVID-19 pandemic in Brazil]] and would not be able to host the fight card.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/com-ibirapuera-cedido-para-hospital-de-campanha-ufc-250-nao-sera-mais-em-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=With Ibirapuera assigned as a field hospital, UFC 250 will not be held in São Paulo|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-08}} {{in lang|pt}} \n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Henry Cejudo]] (also former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] and [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]]) and former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC]] and two-time [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[José Aldo]] was expected to serve as that event's headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28774219/jose-aldo-challenge-henry-cejudo-ufc-250-brazil|title=Jose Aldo to challenge Henry Cejudo at UFC 250 in Brazil|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-02-24|access-date=2020-02-24}} However, Aldo withdrew on April 8 due to [[Travel visa|visa]] issues as the event was expected to be moved to the United States. While not officially announced by the promotion at the time, former two-time bantamweight champion [[Dominick Cruz]] (who hadn't fought since December 2016) was expected to replace him.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/jose-aldo-esta-fora-do-ufc-250-e-ultimate-encaminha-henry-cejudo-x-dominick-cruz.ghtml|title=José Aldo out of UFC 250 and Ultimate expects to book Henry Cejuro against Dominick Cruz|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Ana Hissa and Raphael Marinho|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] (also the current [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]]) and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] [[Felicia Spencer]] was expected to serve as the co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28845108/amanda-nunes-defend-featherweight-title-felicia-spence-ufc-250|title=Amanda Nunes to defend featherweight title against Felicia Spencer at UFC 250|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-03-05}} Nunes announced on April 20 that she would not compete on this date due to her desire to have a full training camp.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228707/amanda-nunes-announces-she-wont-fight-at-may-9-ufc-event|title=Amanda Nunes announces she won't fight at May 9 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}}\n\nDue to the event being expected to be relocated to the United States, several changes were made due to fighters being unable to compete due to visa issues:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/jose-aldo-brazilian-fighters-out-of-ufc-250-visa-issues-coronavirus-pandemic|title=Jose Aldo among Brazilians out of UFC 250 and beyond because of visa issues during pandemic|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}\n\n*A light heavyweight bout between the former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Maurício Rua]] (also [[List of Pride champions#Tournament Winners|2005 PRIDE Middleweight Grand Prix Champion]]) and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] was cancelled. The pairing previously met at [[PRIDE Critical Countdown 2005]] and [[UFC 190]], with Rua winning both fights.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/shogun-e-minotouro-se-enfrentam-pela-terceira-vez-no-ufc-250-em-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=Shogun and Minotouro face off for the third time at UFC 250 in São Paulo|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-02-20|access-date=2020-04-09}} {{in lang|pt}}\n*[[Ketlen Vieira]], who was expected to face [[Marion Reneau]] in a women's bantamweight bout.\n*Former bantamweight title challenger [[Bethe Correia]], who was expected to face [[Pannie Kianzad]] in a women's bantamweight bout.\n*[[Augusto Sakai]], who was expected to face former [[List of WSOF champions#Heavyweight Championship|WSOF Heavyweight Champion]] [[Blagoy Ivanov]] in a heavyweight bout.\n*[[Carlos Felipe]], who was expected to face [[Sergey Spivak]] in a heavyweight bout.\n\n[[File:Jacksonville Veterans Memorial Arena.JPG|thumb|right|230px|[[VyStar Veterans Memorial Arena]] hosted the much anticipated return of the UFC amid the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].]]\n\nOn April 21, it was announced that the UFC moved UFC 249 to the new date, marking the promotion's return after the first wave of cancellations, most likely in Florida. Florida venues are permitted to host UFC events behind closed doors as a result of Executive Order 20-91 signed April 9, 2020 by governor [[Ron DeSantis]], specifically stating as an essential service, \"employees at a professional sports and media production with a national audience - including any athletes, entertainers, production team, executive team, media team and any others necessary to facilitate including services supporting such production - only if the location is closed to the general public.\"{{cite web|url=https://www.flgov.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Essential-Services-Additions-EO-20-91.pdf|title=Essential Services Additions to Executive Order 20-91|publisher=flgov.com|author=State of Florida|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-24}} They confirmed the new card on April 24, with the Ferguson vs. Gaethje bout still scheduled to serve as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29094417/ufc-schedules-three-fight-cards-led-ufc-249-9-florida-no-fans|title=UFC schedules three fight cards, led by UFC 249 on May 9 in Florida with no fans|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-24|access-date=2020-04-24}} The organization also announced that it would host two other events on [[UFC on ESPN: Smith vs. Teixeira|May 13]] and [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Harris|16]], both in Jacksonville. The rest of the card featured the following fights:\n\n*A women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC]] and inaugural [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Carla Esparza]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Michelle Waterson]]. The bout was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]] on April 11, one of the first events cancelled due to the pandemic.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/ufc-portland-michelle-waterson-vs-carla-esparza-booked|title=Michelle Waterson vs. Carla Esparza booked for UFC on ESPN+ 30 in Portland|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2020-02-08|access-date=2020-04-24}}\n*A featherweight bout between [[Bryce Mitchell]] and [[Charles Rosa]]. The bout was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Hermansson vs. Weidman]] a week earlier.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/bryce-mitchell-vs-charles-rosa-in-the-works-for-ufc-oklahoma-city|title=Bryce Mitchell vs. Charles Rosa in the works for UFC Oklahoma City|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-02-26|access-date=2020-04-24}}\n*Bouts already scheduled for the original UFC 250 event on May 9 – the previously mentioned [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between Henry Cejudo and Dominick Cruz, as well as a heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Aleksei Oleinik]].\n*A welterweight rematch between former [[WEC Lightweight Championship|WEC]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Anthony Pettis]] and former lightweight title challenger [[Donald Cerrone]]. The pairing previously met in a lightweight bout at [[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Dodson]] in January 2013, where Pettis won via first round knockout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2013/1/27/3921526/ufc-on-fox-6-results-recap-anthony-pettis-vs-donald-cerrone-fight|title=UFC on Fox 6 results recap: Anthony Pettis vs Donald Cerrone fight review and analysis|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Brian Hemminger|date=2013-01-27|access-date=2020-04-24}}\n\nFights that were originally expected to take place at this event on April 18, but later expected to be rescheduled for future cards included Hernandez vs. Morales, Vera vs. Borg, Worthy vs. Johnson and a women's bantamweight bout between [[Sijara Eubanks]] and [[Sarah Moras]].\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Jeremy Stephens]] weighed in at 150.5 pounds, four and a half pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. He was fined 30% of his purse and his bout with [[Calvin Kattar]] proceeded at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-249-tony-ferguson-justin-gaethje-cejudo-cruz-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC 249 weigh-in results: Title fighters on weight; Jeremy Stephens misses|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-05-08|access-date=2020-05-08}} Additionally, a middleweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]] [[Ronaldo Souza]] and [[Uriah Hall]] was cancelled after Souza and two of his cornermen tested positive for COVID-19.{{cite web|url=https://www.yahoo.com/sports/ronaldo-souza-tests-positive-coronavirus-covid19-pandemic-ufc-249-dana-white-uriah-hall-022308560.html|title=Ronaldo Souza, two cornermen test positive for coronavirus ahead of UFC 249, card will still go on|publisher=yahoo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-05-08|access-date=2020-05-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent on ESPN: Blaydes vs. Volkov]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 251|UFC 251: Usman vs. Masvidal]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 12''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 4''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 27, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/5/21/21266815/ufc-announces-cancellation-of-saskatoon-austin-events|title=UFC announces cancellation of Saskatoon, Austin events|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-05-21|access-date=2020-05-21}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-vegas-4-fight-night-poirier-hooker-results|title=UFC Vegas 4: results|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2020-06-28|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was initially scheduled to take place at the [[Frank Erwin Center]] in [[Austin, Texas]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/8/21169993/ufc-announces-fight-night-card-for-return-to-austin|title=UFC announces Fight Night card for return to Austin|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-03-08|access-date=2020-03-11}} Due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], UFC president [[Dana White]] announced on April 9 that starting with [[UFC 249]], all future events were indefinitely postponed.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises ‘Fight Island’ will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} The event would have marked the promotion's fourth visit to Austin, following [[UFC Fight Night: Cowboy vs. Medeiros]] in February 2018. On May 21, the UFC announced the cancelation of the event in Austin.\n\nA lightweight bout between former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Dan Hooker]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/dustin-poirier-vs-dan-hooker-targeted-for-ufcs-june-27-headliner|title=Dustin Poirier vs. Dan Hooker targeted for UFC's June 27 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-05-23|access-date=2020-06-11}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/12/21289597/ufc-on-espn-12-fight-card-almost-complete-for-june-27-headlined-by-dustin-poirier-vs-dan-hooker|title=UFC on ESPN 12 fight card almost complete for June 27, headlined by Dustin Poirier vs. Dan Hooker|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-06-12|access-date=2020-06-12}} The pairing was previously expected to headline [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Harris|an event on May 16]] in [[San Diego]], [[California]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/3/4/21165300/ufc-dustin-poirier-dan-hooker-headline-ufc-fight-night-175-san-diego-may-16-espn-mma|title=Dustin Poirier vs Dan Hooker targeted to headline UFC Fight Night 175 in San Diego on May 16|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Adam Guillen Jr.|date=2020-03-04|access-date=2020-06-11}} That event was effectively canceled on April 20, after the [[California State Athletic Commission]] (CSAC) extended a moratorium on combat sports events through May 31 due to the COVID-19 pandemic.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228718/ufc-officially-cancels-four-events-due-to-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC officially cancels four events due to coronavirus pandemic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Alexa Grasso]] and [[Ji Yeon Kim]] was initially scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/alexa-grasso-moves-to-flyweight-faces-ji-yeon-kim-at-ufc-austin|title=Alexa Grasso moves up to flyweight, faces Ji Yeon Kim at UFC Austin|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-03-13|access-date=2020-03-13}} However, the bout was rescheduled and would take place on August 29 at [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Rakić]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/ji-yeon-kim-en-alexa-grasso-opnieuw-geboekt-voor-29-augustus-evenement/|title=Ji Yeon Kim and Alexa Grasso re-booked for the August 29 event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-13|access-date=2020-06-15}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n[[Ian Heinisch]] was expected to face [[Brendan Allen]] at the event. However, Heinisch pulled out of the matchup in mid-June citing injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Kyle Daukaus]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/16/21293607/ian-heinisch-out-brendan-allen-now-expected-to-face-ufc-newcomer-kyle-daukaus-on-june-27|title=Ian Heinisch out; Brendan Allen now expected to face UFC newcomer Kyle Daukaus on June 27|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-06-17}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Flyweight Championship|Invicta FC Flyweight Champion]] [[Jennifer Maia]] and [[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]] was initially scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ufc-acerta-luta-entre-as-pesos-moscas-brasileiras-vivi-araujo-e-jennifer-maia-no-texas.ghtml|title=UFC sets fight between Brazilian flyweight Vivi Araújo and Jennifer Maia in Texas|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-02-27|access-date=2020-03-11}} {{in lang|pt}} However, the bout was rescheduled in mid-June and moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan]] on August 1 after both participants faced [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/17/21294793/travel-restrictions-forces-ufc-move-jennifer-maia-viviane-araujo-august|title=Travel restrictions move Jennifer Maia vs. Viviane Araujo to Aug. 1 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|date=2020-06-17|access-date=2020-06-18}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Aspen Ladd]] and former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger (also [[Wrestling at the 2004 Summer Olympics|2004 Olympic silver medalist]] in wrestling) [[Sara McMann]] was scheduled for the event. However, the bout was scrapped after Ladd suffered an injury and was forced to withdraw from the event, tearing both her [[anterior cruciate ligament]] (ACL) and [[medial collateral ligament]] (MCL) in training.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/6/20/21297359/midnight-mania-aspen-ladd-sara-mcmann-ufc-espn-injury-acl|title=Midnight Mania! Aspen Ladd suffers major injury, forced out of Sara McMann fight|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Andrew Richardson|date=2020-06-20|access-date=2020-06-20}}\n\nA catchweight bout between [[Sean Woodson]] and [[Kyle Nelson (fighter)|Kyle Nelson]] was scheduled for the event. However, Nelson was pulled from the event due to a [[Travel visa|visa]] issue and replaced by [[Julian Erosa]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/julian-erosa-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-sean-woodson-aankomende-zaterdag/|title=Julian Erosa takes short notice against Sean Woodson next Saturday|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-24|access-date=2020-06-24}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Miranda Maverick]] and [[Mara Romero Borella]] was scheduled for the event. However, Maverick was forced to pull out due to injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/miranda-maverick-out-of-ufc-on-espn-12-bout-with-mara-romero-borella-due-to-injury|title=Miranda Maverick out of UFC on ESPN 12 bout with Mara Romero Borella due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Nolan King|date=2020-06-24|access-date=2020-06-25}}\n\n[[Ramiz Brahimaj]] was scheduled to face [[Takashi Sato]] at the event. However, Brahimaj was pulled from the fight during the week leading up to the event in an abundance of caution, after a [[cornerman]] of his [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]]. Promotional newcomer [[Jason Witt (fighter)|Jason Witt]] stepped in as a replacement to face Sato.{{cite Jordan Griffin: $20,000\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn August 5, it was announced that the NSAC issued a temporary suspension for [[Luis Peña (fighter)|Luis Peña]], after he tested positive for [[marijuana]] in his pre-fight screenings.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/luis-pena-tim-elliot-jamahal-hill-get-temporary-suspensions-extended-by-nac-after-positive-tests-for-marijuana|title=Three fighters get temporary NAC suspensions extended after positive marijuana tests|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-08-05|access-date=2020-08-05}} On September 3, the NSAC announced that Peña was suspended four and a half months and fined 15% of his fight purse. The reduced suspension was due to his fight being taken on short notice.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29802516/four-ufc-fighters-suspended-fined-positive-drug-tests|title=Four UFC fighters suspended, fined for positive drug tests|publisher=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-03}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|United States}}\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* [[2020 in UFC]]\n\n== References ==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{2020 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:UFC on ESPN]]\n[[Category:2020 in sports in Nevada]]\n[[Category:June 2020 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:2020 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship events in Enterprise, Nevada]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker"}]},{"pageid":63160588,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 251","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2020}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name= UFC 251: Usman vs. Masvidal\n|image= The official poster for UFC 251- Usman vs. Masvidal.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2020|07|12}} \n|venue= [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]]\n|city=[[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates\n|attendance=None ([[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]]){{Cite web|url=https://www.radiotimes.com/news/sport/2020-07-09/inside-ufc-251-fight-island-251-location/|title=Where is UFC Fight Island? Inside UFC 251 location in pictures|publisher=radiotimes.com|author=Thomas Ling|date=2020-07-09|access-date=2020-07-12}}\n|gate=\n|buyrate= 1,300,000{{Cite web|url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/1926427/2020/07/13/sources-ufc-251-generates-around-1-3-million-ppv-buys-most-since-2018/|title=Sources: UFC 251 generates around 1.3 million PPV buys, most since 2018|publisher=theathletic.com|author=Mike Coppinger|date=2020-07-13|access-date=2020-07-14}}\n|previousevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 251: Usman vs. Masvidal''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 12, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|title=UFC 'Fight Island' is ... Yas Island, Abu Dhabi|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-fight-island-located-yas-island-abu-dhabi|access-date=2020-06-09|website=MMA Junkie|publisher=USA Today, [[Gannett Company]]}} It was originally planned to take place on June 6 at [[Perth Arena]] in [[Perth]], Australia.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/perth-australia-announced-as-venue-for-ufc-251|title=Valentina Shevchenko vs. Joanne Calderwood title fight heads to UFC 251 in Perth, Australia|date=2020-02-25|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|access-date=2020-02-25}} Due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], the event was eventually postponed on April 9 ''(see section below)''.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises 'Fight Island' will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} In early June, it was confirmed that the event would instead take place in Abu Dhabi.\n\n==Background==\nThe event would have marked the promotion's second visit to Perth, following [[UFC 221]] in February 2018. However, the promotion later announced on May 5 that it would officially postpone the realization of this event in Perth due to the COVID-19 pandemic.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-officially-pulls-plug-perths-ufc-251-date|title=UFC officially pulls plug on Perth's UFC 251 date|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-05-05|access-date=2020-05-07}}\n\nWhile not officially announced by the organization, a [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former champion [[Max Holloway]] was originally expected to headline the event on June 6.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/2/25/21153823/report-alex-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-rematch-headline-ufc-251-mma-news|title=Alex Volkanovski vs. Max Holloway rematch to headline UFC 251|date=2020-02-25|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|access-date=2020-02-26|archive-date=2020-04-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200406052123/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/2/25/21153823/report-alex-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-rematch-headline-ufc-251-mma-news|url-status=dead}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 245]], where Volkanovski defeated Holloway via unanimous decision to capture the title.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/12/ufc-245-results-alexander-volkanovski-dethrones-max-holloway-captures-ufc-featherweight-belt|title=UFC 245 results: Alexander Volkanovski dethrones Max Holloway, captures UFC featherweight belt|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2019-12-15|access-date=2019-12-15}} Volkanovski indicated in late March that due to the COVID-19 pandemic, he would postpone his title defense on the initial June 6 date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28968938/alexander-volkanovski-puts-ufc-title-defense-ice-amid-coronavirus-pandemic|title=Alexander Volkanovski puts UFC title defense on ice amid coronavirus pandemic|work=espn.com|author=Sam Bruce|date=2020-03-29|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and [[Joanne Calderwood]] was expected to take place on the event's first date.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/valentina-shevchenko-joanne-calderwood-flyweight-title-ufc-251-june|title=Champ Valentina Shevchenko meets Joanne Calderwood at UFC 251 in June|date=2020-02-20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|access-date=2020-02-20}} However, Shevchenko was forced to withdraw from the event on March 31 due to a leg injury and the bout was postponed.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-251-valentina-shevchenko-injured-joanne-calderwood-fight-postponed|title=Valentina Shevchenko injured; UFC 251 title fight vs. Joanne Calderwood on hold|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-03-31|access-date=2020-03-31}}\n\n[[File:Yas Marina Circuit + Ferrari World -Abu Dhabi.jpg|thumb|right|190px|[[Yas Island]] was also the site for three previous UFC events in [[Abu Dhabi]]. One of them, [[UFC Fight Night: Nogueira vs. Nelson]], was held at [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Arena]]. The other two Yas Island events, [[UFC 112]] and [[UFC 242]], were held at temporary venues.]]\n\n===COVID-19 pandemic, relocation to Abu Dhabi===\nUFC president [[Dana White]] stated on April 7 that he was pursuing an international venue — described as a \"[[private island]]\" or \"Fight Island\" — to facilitate the hosting of events involving fighters impacted by U.S. [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic|travel restrictions]].{{cite web|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-07|title=Dana White's COVID-19 plan: Two months of weekly fights at UFC 249 location, a private island, and Joe Rogan|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/7/21211634/dana-white-covid-19-plans-weekly-fights-at-ufc-249-location-a-private-island-and-joe-rogan|access-date=2020-05-10|website=[[MMAFighting.com]]|publisher=[[Vox Media]]}}{{cite web|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-07|title=UFC's White: Secured private island for fights|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29006529/dana-white-secured-us-location-private-island-ufc-fights|access-date=2020-05-10|website=ESPN|publisher=}}\n\nOn June 9, the UFC revealed that the \"[[Fight Island]]\" site would be Yas Island, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates and that it would also host four events in July. This event was the fourth UFC event held on Yas Island overall, succeeding [[UFC 242]] in September 2019. Additionally, there is a 10 square mile (16 km) \"safety zone\" on the island restricted to essential personnel. The designated area includes access to amenities such as hotels, restaurants and a training cage on the beach.{{Cite web|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-06-09|title=White: Fight Island is in UAE, will host UFC 251|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29287612/ufc-fight-island-host-events-next-month|access-date=2020-06-09|website=ESPN.com|publisher=}} The fights took place inside the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] — an indoor events arena. It does not have communal areas and those entering the site must go through \"mist tunnels\" to disinfect themselves from surface bacteria.{{Cite web|author=Kevin Iole|date=2020-07-07|title=Abu Dhabi's 'Fight Island' could become a lot more familiar if COVID-19 shuts down Las Vegas again|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/abu-dhabis-fight-island-could-become-a-lot-more-familiar-if-covid-19-shuts-las-vegas-down-again-210850274.html|access-date=2020-07-10|website=[[Yahoo! Sports]]|publisher=}}{{Cite web|author=Ben Collins|date=2020-07-10|title=UFC 251: 'Beautiful, surreal, insane' - what's Fight Island really like?|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/53357610|access-date=2020-07-10|website=[[BBC Sport]]|publisher=}}\n\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to worry about scheduling the event for local primetime hours. The event was instead scheduled for [[prime time]] in the U.S. [[Eastern Time Zone]]; the full preliminary card began at approximately 2:00 am (July 12) [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|author=Wagenheim|first=Jeff|date=2020-06-11|title=What we know and don't know about 'Fight Island'|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29294243/ufc-251-know-know-fight-island|access-date=2020-06-23|website=ESPN.com|publisher=}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Kamaru Usman]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) and [[Gilbert Burns]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-251-kamaru-usman-vs-gilbert-burns-booked-to-headline|title=Kamaru Usman vs. Gilbert Burns headlines UFC 251 on 'Fight Island' in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-09}} However, it was announced on July 3 that Burns was pulled from the contest after he and his coach, Greg Jones, [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-251-gilbert-burns-out-kamaru-usman-title-fight-coronavirus|title=Gilbert Burns tests positive for COVID-19, out of UFC 251 headliner vs. Kamaru Usman|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} Pending pre-fight testing and a 24-hour quarantine, [[Jorge Masvidal]] was expected to serve as his replacement.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-251-kamaru-usman-vs-jorge-masvidal-welterweight-title-booked-fight-island|title=Jorge Masvidal steps in on six days' notice to fight Kamaru Usman at UFC 251|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and John Morgan|date=2020-07-05|access-date=2020-07-05}} After he passed his initial test, the new main event was confirmed.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-251-kamaru-usman-vs-jorge-masvidal-title-fight-official-covid-19-test|title=Jorge Masvidal passes COVID-19 test; UFC makes Kamaru Usman title fight official|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-06|access-date=2020-07-06}}\n\nThe previously mentioned UFC Featherweight Championship rematch between Volkanovski and Holloway co-headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-25q-alexander-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-rematch-booked-fight-island|title=Jorge Masvidal passes COVID-19 test; UFC and [[Rose Namajunas]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/rose-namajunas-vs-jessica-andrade-rematch-booked-for-ufcs-july-11-card|title=Rose Namajunas vs. Jessica Andrade rematch Andrade won via second-round TKO to capture the title.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmaindia.com/ufc-237-results-jessica-andrade-kos-rose-namajunas-in-round-2-wins-strawweight-belt/|title=UFC 237 results – Jessica Andrade KO's Rose Namajunas in round 2, wins strawweight belt|publisher=mmaindia.com|author=Sohail Syed|date=2019-05-12|access-date=2020-06-09}} They were originally expected to co-headline UFC 249.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/ufc-249-rose-namajunas-vs-jessica-andrade-rematch-booked|title=Rose Namajunas, Jessica Andrade agree to rematch at UFC 249 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-01-09|access-date=2020-06-09}} However, Namajunas pulled out for personal reasons, with her manager citing a pair of deaths in the family related to the COVID-19 pandemic as the reason.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/29013557/rose-namajunas-pulls-ufc-249-bout-vs-jessica-andrade|title=Rose Namajunas pulls deaths in family|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n*A heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and [[Ciryl Gane]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/shamil-abdurakhimov-vs-ciryl-gane-tijdens-ufc-251s-ifw-week-evenement/|title=Shamil Abdurakhimov vs. Ciryl Gane at UFC 251's IFW week Abdurakhimov was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons. In turn, Gane was rescheduled to fight [[Sergei Pavlovich]] at [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs Oleinik]].{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/sergei-pavlovich-treft-ciryl-gane-tijdens-8-augustus-evenement/|title=Sergei Pavlovich encounters Ciryl Gane during the August 8 event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-04-14|access-date=2020-06-15}} {{in lang|nl}}\n*A women's strawweight bout between [[Amanda Ribas]] and [[Paige VanZant]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/luta-entre-amanda-ribas-e-paige-vanzant-e-remarcada-pra-11-de-julho-pelo-ufc.ghtml|title=Fight between Amanda Ribas and Paige VanZant is rescheduled for July 11 by UFC|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-05-21|access-date=2020-06-09}} {{in lang|pt}} They were initially scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Lee vs. Oliveira]] in March. However, VanZant was forced to pull out of the fight due to an undisclosed injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Paige-VanZant-Out-Randa-Markos-In-Against-Amanda-Ribas-at-UFC-Brasilia-169165|title=Report: Paige VanZant out, Randa Markos in against Amanda Ribas at UFC Brasilia|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2020-01-24|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Pedro Munhoz]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] was briefly linked to the event. However, promotion officials elected to schedule the pairing for [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]] four days later.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/update-pedro-munhoz-vs-frankie-edgar-moved-to-ufc-event-on-july-15th/|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Frankie Edgar moved to UFC event on July 15th|publisher=fightbookmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-06-16}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Marcin Tybura]] between [[Alexander Romanov (fighter)|Alexander Romanov]] was scheduled for the event. However, Romanov was pulled from the event after testing positive for COVID-19 and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Maxim Grishin]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/voormalig-wfca-kampioen-maxim-grishin-maakt-ufc-debuut-op-11-juli-tijdens-ufc-251/|title=Former WFCA champion Maxim Grishin makes UFC debut on July 11 at UFC 251|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-03}} {{in lang|nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/gym-says-alexander-romanov-removed-ufc-positive-covid-19-test-coronavirus|title=Gym: Alexander Romanov was removed from UFC 251 due to positive COVID-19 tests|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-09|access-date=2020-07-09}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Raulian Paiva]] and [[Vanessa Melo]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Paiva weighed in at 129 pounds, three pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. Melo weighed in at 141 pounds, five pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were each fined 20% and 30% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents [[Zhalgas Zhumagulov]] and [[Karol Rosa]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/10/21319912/ufc-251-weigh-in-results-kamaru-usman-jorge-masvidal-make-weight-all-title-fights-official|title=UFC 251 weigh-in results: Kamaru Usman, Jorge Masvidal make weight, all title fights official|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K.
