251|UFC 251: Usman vs. Masvidal]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 13''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 1''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 16, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-fight-island-located-yas-island-abu-dhabi|title=UFC 'Fight Island' is ... Yas Island, Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the second of four UFC [[Fight Island]] events scheduled to take place on Yas Island in July 2020, following [[UFC 251]], as part of a plan to facilitate the hosting of events involving fighters impacted by U.S. [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].\n\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion didn't have to worry about the local timing of the event, so it proceeded with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card began at 6:00 am (July 16) local time in Abu Dhabi, and a full preliminary card began at approximately 3:00 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29294243/ufc-251-know-know-fight-island|title=What we know and don't know about 'Fight Island'|publisher=espn.com|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2020-06-11|access-date=2020-06-23}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Calvin Kattar]] and [[Dan Ige]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29316565/sources-calvin-kattar-dan-ige-headline-ufc-fight-night-july-15-fight-island|title=Calvin Kattar, Dan Ige to headline UFC Fight Night on July 15 at Fight Island|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-06-15|access-date=2020-06-16}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[John Phillips (fighter)|John Phillips]] and [[Duško Todorović]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Woodley vs. Edwards]]. However, the event was the first of five to be cancelled due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]] and the bout was moved to [[Cage Warriors]] 113 (as the English organization's president decided to help some of the scheduled bouts to happen in his promotion's event scheduled for March 20).{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/some-ufc-on-espn-plus-29-london-fights-might-happen-at-cage-warriors-113|title=Graham Boylan hopes to save some UFC on ESPN+ 29 fights in London with Cage Warriors 113|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-06-18}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-london-john-phillips-vs-dusko-todorovic-added-to-cage-warriors-113|title=UFC London's John Phillips vs. Dusko Todorovic added to Cage Warriors 113|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-03-16|access-date=2020-06-18}} In turn, Todorović pulled out due to other travel restrictions in the region.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/cage-warriors-113-bartosz-fabinski-darren-stewart-new-main-event|title=UFC's Bartosz Fabinski, Darren Stewart headline reshuffled Cage Warriors 113 card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-03-18|access-date=2020-03-19}} They were then expected to meet at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/mixed-martial-arts/53101785|title=UFC Fight Island: Welsh pair Brett Johns and John Phillips added to series|publisher=bbc.co.uk|author=Staff|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} On July 8, Todorović pulled out once again, this time due to a potential medical issue.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-fight-island-dusko-todorovic-out-john-phillips-needs-opponent|title=Dusko Todorovic out at UFC on ESPN 13; John Phillips needs new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2020-07-08|access-date=2020-07-08}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Khamzat Chimaev]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/khamzat-chimaev-tapped-as-replacement-to-fight-john-phillips-at-ufc-on-espn-13|title=Khamzat Chimaev tapped as replacement to fight John Phillips at UFC on ESPN 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-07-08|access-date=2020-07-08}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Pedro Munhoz]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] was briefly linked to [[UFC 251]], four days earlier. However, promotion officials elected to schedule the pairing for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/update-pedro-munhoz-vs-frankie-edgar-moved-to-ufc-event-on-july-15th/|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Frankie Edgar moved to UFC event on July 15th|publisher=fightbookmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-06-18}} On July 6, it was announced that the bout between Munhoz and Edgar was scrapped due to Munhoz testing positive for COVID-19.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/6/21315428/pedro-munhoz-tests-positive-covid-19-ufc-fight-island-frankie-edgar/|title=Pedro Munhoz tests positive for COVID-19, pulled from UFC ‘Fight Island’ bout with Frankie Edgar|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-07-06|access-date=2020-07-06|archive-date=2020-07-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200707084948/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2020/7/6/21315428/pedro-munhoz-tests-positive-covid-19-ufc-fight-island-frankie-edgar/|url-status=dead}} They were rescheduled for [[UFC 252]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/news/1984875|title=Edgar-Munhoz rescheduled for UFC 252|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2020-07-12}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Vinicius Moreira]] and [[Modestas Bukauskas]] was scheduled for the event, along with another bout between [[Anderson dos Santos (fighter)|Anderson dos Santos]] and [[Jack Shore]] at bantamweight. However, Moreira and dos Santos both [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] on July 3 and were removed from the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Anderson-dos-Santos-Vinicius-Moreira-Out-of-July-15-Fight-Island-Card-After-Positive-COVID19-Tests-173766|title=Anderson dos Santos, Vinicius Moreira out of July 15 \"Fight Island\" card after positive COVID-19 tests|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tudor Leonte|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} They were replaced by [[Andreas Michailidis]] and [[Aaron Phillips (fighter)|Aaron Phillips]] respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/modestas-bukauskas-remains-on-ufc-fight-island-card-meets-andreas-michailidis-on-short-notice/|title=Modestas Bukauskas remains on UFC Fight Island card, meets Andreas Michailidis on short-notice|publisher=fightbookmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2020-07-04|access-date=2020-07-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-fight-island-aaron-phillips-jack-shore-july-15|title=Aaron Phillips set for UFC return, faces Jack Shore on 'Fight Island'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Simon Head|date=2020-07-04|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC]] and [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Carla Esparza]] and [[Marina Rodriguez (fighter)|Marina Rodriguez]] was scheduled for the event. However, a Rodriguez [[cornerman]] tested positive for COVID-19 and the bout was cancelled. Rodriguez had her test come back negative, but the bout was scrapped due to precautionary reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/marina-rodriguez-vs-carla-esparza-removed-from-july-15-ufc-event-after-positive-covid-19-test|title=Marina Rodriguez vs. Carla Esparza removed from July 15 UFC event after positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Nolan King|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} They were rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till]] on July 26.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/carla-esparza-vs-marina-rodriguez-rebooked-july-ufc-abu-dhabi-fight-island-coronavirus|title=Carla Esparza vs. Marina Rodriguez reset for July 25 UFC show in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-05|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between Timo Feucht and Kenneth Bergh was scheduled for the event. However, one day after the bout was announced, Feucht was removed from the event for unknown reasons and was replaced by Jorge Gonzalez.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jorge-gonzalez-vervangt-timo-feucht-tegen-kenneth-bergh-op-15-juli-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Jorge Gonzalez replaces Timo Feucht against Kenneth Bergh on July 15 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-10|access-date=2020-07-10}} {{in lang|nl}} It was later announced that Feucht was removed due to past [[Neo-Nazism|Neo-Nazi]] ties, including a January 2016 attack in the Connewitz district of [[Leipzig]] for which he was arrested.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/11/21318073/ufc-timo-feucht-fight-island-releases-fighter-alleged-past-neo-nazi-ties-mma|title=UFC rescinds offer to Timo Feucht for Fight Island bout over alleged past Neo-Nazi ties|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|date=2020-07-11|access-date=2020-07-11}} In turn, the bout was cancelled on the day of the weigh-ins as Bergh had issues during his weight cut.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-on-espn-13-kenneth-bergh-jorge-gonzalez-off|title=Weight cut issues for Kenneth Bergh force cancellation of Jorge Gonzalez fight at UFC on ESPN 13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-14|access-date=2020-07-14}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Abdul Razak Alhassan]] and [[Chris Fishgold]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Alhassan weighed in at 174 pounds, three pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Fishgold weighed in at 149 pounds, three pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. Both their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were respectively fined 20% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents [[Mounir Lazzez]] and [[Jared Gordon]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/14/21323978/ufc-on-espn-13-weigh-in-results-two-miss-weight-one-fight-canceled|title= UFC on ESPN 13 weigh-in results: Two miss weight, one fight canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. ESPN: Blaydes vs. Volkov\n|image= UFC Fight Night 177 official poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2020|06|20}}\n|venue= [[UFC Apex]]\n|city= [[Enterprise, Nevada]], United States\n|attendance=None ([[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]]){{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/ufc-on-espn-11-bonuses-emmett-burgos-earn-fight-of-the-night-honors/|title=UFC on ESPN 11 bonuses: Emmett & Burgos earn fight of the night honors|author=Fernando Quiles Jr.|publisher=mmanews.com|date=2020-06-21|access-date=2020-06-21}}\n|gate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Eye vs. Calvillo]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Blaydes vs. Volkov''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 11''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 3''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 20, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite news|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Announces-Blaydes-vs-Volkov-Will-Remain-Headliner-for-June-20-Card-173157|title=UFC Announces Blaydes vs. Volkov will remain headliner for June 20 card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-05-31|access-date=2020-06-02}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-vegas-3-results-fight-night-blaydes-volkov|title=UFC Vegas 3: results|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2020-06-21|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was initially scheduled to take place at the [[SaskTel Centre]] in [[Saskatoon]], [[Saskatchewan]], Canada.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/27/21156455/ufc-heads-to-saskatoon-on-june-20-for-espn-event|title=UFC heads to Saskatoon on June 20 for ESPN+ event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-02-27|access-date=2020-03-03}} Due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], UFC president [[Dana White]] announced on April 9 that starting with [[UFC 249]], all future events were indefinitely postponed.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises ‘Fight Island’ will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} The event would have marked the promotion's second visit to Saskatoon, following [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Oliveira]] in August 2015. On May 21, the UFC announced the cancelation of the event in Canada.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/5/21/21266815/ufc-announces-cancellation-of-saskatoon-austin-events|title=UFC announces cancellation of Saskatoon, Austin events|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-04-21|access-date=2020-04-21}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Curtis Blaydes]] and former [[List of Bellator champions#Heavyweight Championship|Bellator Heavyweight Champion]] [[Alexander Volkov (fighter)|Alexander Volkov]] served as the event headliner.\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[List of Bellator champions#Welterweight Championship|Bellator Welterweight Champion]] [[Lyman Good]] and [[Belal Muhammad]] was previously expected to take place at [[UFC 205]] in November 2016, but Good was pulled from that event after being notified by the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) due to a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition drug test.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/lyman-good-receives-usada-notice-pulled-from-ufc-205|title=Lyman Good receives USADA notice, pulled from UFC 205|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2016-10-24|access-date=2020-06-16}} The matchup was once again booked for UFC 249 in April 2020, but Good pulled out due to testing positive for COVID-19, being the first fighter to publicly acknowledge the fact.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29073417/ufc-lyman-good-now-100-testing-positive-covid-19-last-month|title=UFC's Lyman Good now '100%' after testing positive for COVID-19 last month|publisher=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-06-16}} They ultimately met at this event instead.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/lyman-good-returns-covid-19-infection-vs-belal-muhammad-june-20-ufc-event|title=Lyman Good to face Belal Muhammad in first UFC fight since coronavirus infection|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-05-22|access-date=2020-06-16}}\n\n[[Joe Solecki]] was expected to face [[Austin Hubbard]] at the event. However, Solecki was removed from the pairing on June 13 for undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/joe-solecki-announces-hes-out-of-ufc-on-espn-11-fight-vs-austin-hubbard|title=Joe Solecki announces he's out of UFC on ESPN 11 fight vs. Austin Hubbard|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-13|access-date=2020-06-15}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Max Rohskopf.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/max-rohskopf-agrees-to-face-austin-hubbard-at-ufc-on-espn-11|title=Max Rohskopf agrees to face Austin Hubbard at UFC on ESPN 11|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan and Nolan King|date=2020-06-14|access-date=2020-06-15}}\n\n[[Matt Frevola]] was scheduled to face [[Frank Camacho]] at the event. However, Frevola was pulled from the fight during the week leading up to the event after teammate and [[cornerman]], [[Billy Quarantillo]], [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]]. Despite two negative tests, Frevola was removed from the bout in an abundance of caution.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-espn-11-matt-frevola-out-billy-quarantillo-positive-covid-test|title=Matt Frevola out of UFC on ESPN 11 after cornerman Billy Quarantillo tests positive for COVID-19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} Promotional newcomer [[Justin Jaynes]] was tabbed as a replacement to face Camacho.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/18/21295679/with-matt-frevola-out-frank-camacho-welcomes-newcomer-justin-jaynes-at-ufc-on-espn-11|title=With Matt Frevola out, Frank Camacho welcomes newcomer Justin Jaynes at UFC on ESPN 11|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Frank Camacho]] weighed in at 158 pounds, two pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at a catchweight and Camacho was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent Justin Jaynes.{{cite web|url=https://dknation.draftkings.com/ufc-mma-mixed-martial-arts-cards-ppv/2020/6/19/21297087/frank-camacho-vs-justin-jaynes-weigh-in-news-misses-weight-ufc-on-espn-11|title=Frank Camacho misses weight against replacement fight for UFC on ESPN 11|publisher=dknation.draftkings.com|author=David Fucillo|date=2020-06-19|access-date=2020-06-19}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Curtis Blaydes]]\n|def.\n|[[Alexander Volkov (fighter)|Alexander Volkov]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–46, 48–47, 48–46)\n|5\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Josh Emmett]]\n|def.\n|[[Shane Burgos]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Bantamweight\n|[[Raquel Pennington]]\n|def.\n|[[Marion Reneau]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Belal Muhammad]]\n|def.\n|[[Lyman Good]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (160 lb)\n|[[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]]\n|def.\n|[[Roosevelt Roberts]]\n|Submission (armbar)\n|1\n|2:25\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN / ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Bobby Green]]\n|def.\n|[[Clay Guida]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Tecia Torres]]\n|def.\n|[[Brianna Van Buren]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Marc-André Barriault]]\n|vs.\n|Oskar Piechota\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|2\n|4:50 \n|Originally a TKO (punches) win for Barriault; It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $1,655,000.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/21/21298012/ufc-on-espn-11-salaries-jim-miller-top-earner-with-208000-payout|title=UFC on ESPN 11 salaries: Jim Miller top earner with $208,000 payout|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-06-21|access-date=2020-06-21}}\n\n* '''Curtis Blaydes:''' $180,000 (includes $90,000 win bonus) def. Marion Reneau: $38,000\n* '''Belal Muhammad:''' $100,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Lyman Good: $28,000\n* '''Jim Miller:''' $208,000 (includes $104,000 win bonus) def. Roosevelt Roberts: $25,000\n* '''Bobby Green:''' $72,000 (includes $36,000 win bonus) def. Clay Guida: $73,000\n* '''Tecia Torres:''' $96,000 (includes $48,000 win bonus) def. Cortney Casey: $53,000\n* '''Justin Jaynes:''' $29,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Frank Camacho: $20,000 ^\n* '''Lauren Murphy:''' $76,000 (includes $38,000 win bonus) def. Roxanne Modafferi: $50,000\n* '''Austin Hubbard:''' $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Max Rohskopf: $12,000\n\n^ ''Camacho ($5,000) was fined 20% of his purse for failing to make the required weight limit for his fight. That money was issued to his opponent, a NSAC official confirmed.''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn August 5, it was announced that the NSAC issued a temporary suspension for [[Marc-André Barriault]], after he tested positive for [[ostarine]] in his drug test associated with the event. The suspension would hold until a full disciplinary hearing at the next NSAC meeting, expected to take place in September.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/marc-andre-barriault-disappointed-in-ostarine-finding-but-vows-to-prove-innocence|title=Marc-Andre Barriault 'disappointed' in ostarine finding but vows to prove innocence|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2020-08-05|access-date=2020-08-05}} Four months later, Barriault was officially suspended for nine months retroactive to the date of the fight, with the result of the bout being overturned to a no contest due to the violation. He was fined $2,100 and before he is re-licensed in Las Vegas, Barriault will also have to pay a prosecution fee of $254.38.\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|United States}}\n* [[List of UFC in [[São Paulo]], Brazil.{{Cite web|url=https://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/ufc-249-khabib-nurmagomedov-tony-ferguson-m55867|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20191214111050/https://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/ufc-249-khabib-nurmagomedov-tony-ferguson-1.39502080|url-status=live|archive-date=December 14, 2019|title=Khabib vs. Ferguson set for UFC 249 at Barclays|publisher=newsday.com|author=Mark La Monica|date=2019-12-14|access-date=2019-12-14}} Due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], the event was eventually postponed ''(see section below)''.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29018339/dana-white-says-ufc-249-not-happen-april-18|title=Dana White says UFC 249 will not happen April 18|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} On April 21, the UFC confirmed that [[UFC 249]] would be moved to May 9 and UFC 250 was pushed back to June 6.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/21/21230050/ufc-249-gets-second-life-as-may-9-event-at-TBA-location|title=UFC 249 gets second life as May 9 event at TBA Heck|date=2020-04-21|access-date=2020-04-21}} In late May, the promotion confirmed the event would take place in Las Vegas.\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Henry Cejudo]] (also former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] and [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]]) and former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC]] and two-time [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[José Aldo]] was expected to serve as the initial event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28774219/jose-aldo-challenge-henry-cejudo-ufc-250-brazil|title=Jose Aldo to challenge Henry Cejudo at UFC 250 in Brazil|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-02-24|access-date=2020-02-24}} However, Aldo pulled out on April 8 due to [[Travel visa|visa]] issues as the event was expected to be moved to the United States ''(see section below)''. Former two-time bantamweight champion [[Dominick Cruz]] replaced Aldo.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/jose-aldo-esta-fora-do-ufc-250-e-ultimate-encaminha-henry-cejudo-x-dominick-cruz.ghtml|title=José Aldo out of UFC 250 and Ultimate expects to book Henry Cejuro against Dominick Cruz|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Ana Hissa and Raphael Marinho|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] (also the current [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]]) and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] [[Felicia Spencer]] was expected to serve as the co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/28845108/amanda-nunes-defend-featherweight-title-felicia-spence-ufc-250|title=Amanda Nunes to defend featherweight title against Felicia Spencer at UFC 250|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-03-05}} Nunes announced on April 20 that she would not compete at a new proposed event for May 9 due to her desire to have a full training camp.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228707/amanda-nunes-announces-she-wont-fight-at-may-9-ufc-event|title=Amanda Nunes announces she won't fight at May 9 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}}\n\n===COVID-19 pandemic===\nThe event was originally expected to take place at Ginásio do Ibirapuera in São Paulo, Brazil. On April 7, it was announced that the venue will be used as a [[field hospital]] during the COVID-19 pandemic and would not be able to host the fight card.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/com-ibirapuera-cedido-para-hospital-de-campanha-ufc-250-nao-sera-mais-em-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=With Ibirapuera assigned as a field hospital, UFC 250 will not be held in São Paulo|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-04-07|access-date=2020-04-08}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nDue to the event being expected to be relocated to the United States, several changes were made due to fighters being unable to compete due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]]:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/jose-aldo-brazilian-fighters-out-of-ufc-250-visa-issues-coronavirus-pandemic|title=Jose Aldo among Brazilians out of UFC 250 and beyond because of visa issues during pandemic|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}\n\n*A light heavyweight bout between the former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Maurício Rua]] (also [[List of Pride champions#Tournament Winners|2005 PRIDE Middleweight Grand Prix Champion]]) and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] was cancelled. The pairing previously met at [[PRIDE Critical Countdown 2005]] and [[UFC 190]], with Rua winning both fights.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/shogun-e-minotouro-se-enfrentam-pela-terceira-vez-no-ufc-250-em-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=Shogun and Minotouro face off for the third time at UFC 250 in São Paulo|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-02-20|access-date=2020-04-09}} {{in lang|pt}} The bout was later rescheduled for July 25, at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/dana-white-ufc-books-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-vs-mauricio-rua-3-for-july-25|title=Dana White: UFC books Antonio Rogerio Nogueira vs. Mauricio Rua 3 for July 25|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-04|access-date=2020-06-05}}\n*[[Ketlen Vieira]], who was expected to face [[Marion Reneau]] in a women's bantamweight bout.\n*Former title challenger [[Bethe Correia]], who was expected to face [[Pannie Kianzad]] in a women's bantamweight bout. This fight was also rescheduled for UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-planeja-remarcar-luta-entre-bethe-correia-e-pannie-kianzad-para-o-mes-de-julho.ghtml|title=Ultimate plans to reschedule fight between Bethe Correia and Pannie Kianzad for July, on Fight Island|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone|date=2020-05-23|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|pt}}\n*[[Augusto Sakai]], who was expected to face former [[List of WSOF champions#Heavyweight Championship|WSOF Heavyweight Champion]] [[Blagoy Ivanov]] in a heavyweight bout. This fight was later rescheduled for May 30, at [[UFC on ESPN: Woodley vs. Burns]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-on-espn-9-dana-white-announces-complete-lineup-woodley-vs-burns|title=Dana White announces full UFC on ESPN 9 lineup for May 30 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-05-22|access-date=2020-05-23}}\n*[[Carlos Felipe]], who was expected to face [[Sergey Spivak]] in a heavyweight bout. The bout was later rescheduled for July 18, at [[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]].{{Cite web|url=http://mma.uno/sergey-spivak-vs-carlos-felipe-transferido-a-fight-island-del-18-de-julio/|title=Sergey Spivak vs. Carlos Felipe transferred to Fight Island on July 18|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2020-06-10|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nUFC president [[Dana White]] announced on April 9 that starting with UFC 249, all future events were indefinitely postponed.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises 'Fight Island' will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} It was confirmed that this event was cancelled on April 20, though the UFC was still targeting a new event for the same date (not expected to be the original UFC 250).{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228718/ufc-officially-cancels-four-events-due-to-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC officially cancels four events due to coronavirus pandemic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}} The UFC moved the previously mentioned UFC Bantamweight Championship bout between Cejudo and Cruz, as well as a heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Aleksei Oleinik]] to the new UFC 249 date.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29094417/ufc-schedules-three-fight-cards-led-ufc-249-9-florida-no-fans|title=UFC schedules three fight cards, led by UFC 249 on May 9 in Florida with no fans|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-24|access-date=2020-04-24}}\n\nOn May 10, it was confirmed that UFC 250 was going to take place on June 6 in the United States, with the previously mentioned UFC Women's Featherweight Championship bout between Nunes and Spencer expected to headline the event. This event also featured fighters that were pulled from other events previously cancelled.\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Florida|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-05-01|access-date=2020-05-02}}\n\n==Background==\nDue to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], the UFC had to cancel two events and postpone four events between [[UFC Fight Night: Woodley vs. Edwards|March 21]] and May 2.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-postpones-three-events-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC postpones three events amid growing coronavirus outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-03-16}}{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29018339/dana-white-says-ufc-249-not-happen-april-18|title=Dana White says UFC 249 will not happen April 18|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228718/ufc-officially-cancels-four-events-due-to-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC officially cancels four events due to coronavirus pandemic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}} On April 24, they announced their return of [[UFC 249]] in Jacksonville, [[Florida]], with two other events scheduled for the same venue on May 13 and [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Harris|May 16]] respectively.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29094417/ufc-schedules-three-fight-cards-led-ufc-249-9-florida-no-fans|title=UFC schedules three fight cards, led by UFC 249 on May 9 in Florida with no fans|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-04-24|access-date=2020-04-24}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] served as the event headliner. They were originally scheduled to headline [[Canceled UFC event on April 25, 2020|another event on April 25]], but it was cancelled due to the ongoing pandemic.\n\nThe event included fighters that were pulled from other events previously cancelled, as well as the following bouts:\n\n*A middleweight bout between [[Karl Roberson]] and [[Marvin Vettori]] (also scheduled for the cancelled event on April 25 in [[Lincoln, Nebraska]]).\n*A heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Andrei Arlovski]] and [[Philipe Lins]] (scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Hermansson vs. Weidman]]).\n*A women's bantamweight bout between [[Sijara Eubanks]] and [[Sarah Moras]] (scheduled for the original UFC 249 on April 18 in [[Brooklyn]], [[New York (state)|New York]]).\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Roberson weighed in at 187.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the middleweight non-title fight limit. He was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent Vettori and the bout was expected to proceed at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29165692/anthony-smith-glover-teixeira-make-weight-wednesday-bout|title=Anthony Smith, Glover Teixeira make weight for Wednesday Championship]] that took place on May 30, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/dana-white-says-ufc-may-30-event-held-apex-las-vegas|title=Dana White says UFC's May 30 event to be held at Apex in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-05-20|access-date=2020-05-21}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-vegas-results-woodley-burns-apex|title=UFC Vegas results|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2020-05-31|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\n==Background==\nAmid the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], this event was originally expected to be held on May 23, but eventually was pushed back to May 30 at a yet [[To be determined|TBD]] location.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-targets-may-30-event-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC eyes May 30 as new date for event originally planned May 23|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-05-12|access-date=2020-05-13}} UFC president [[Dana White]] stated that he intended for the event to take place in Las Vegas. However, if the [[Government of Nevada|Nevada state government]] did not allow the fight to happen due to restrictions put in place in response to the COVID-19 pandemic, he would look to have the event take place in [[Arizona]] instead, since professional sports were allowed to return on May 15 without fans in attendance.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Dana-White-Arizona-Targeted-for-May-30-UFC-Event-if-Las-Vegas-Isnt-Open-172921|title=Dana White: Arizona targeted for May 30 UFC event if Las Vegas isn't open|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-05-17|access-date=2020-05-17}} White confirmed on May 20 that the event would be held at the UFC Apex facility in Las Vegas, despite no official announcement from the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC) regarding permission for combat sports. The NSAC approved the return of mixed martial arts events to the state on May 27.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/nevada-athletic-commission-approves-las-vegas-ufc-events-covid-19-coronavirus|title=Nevada Athletic Commission approves first Las Vegas UFC events since COVID-19 outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-05-27|access-date=2020-05-27}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Tyron Woodley]] and [[Gilbert Burns]] was long rumored to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|title=Tyron Woodley to fight Gilbert Burns at UFC Fight Night on May 30|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29194139/sources-tyron-woodley-fight-gilbert-burns-ufc-fight-night-30|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-05-19|publisher=espn.com|access-date=2020-05-19}} On May 22, Dana White confirmed the main event and entire card to be broadcast on [[ESPN]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-on-espn-9-dana-white-announces-complete-lineup-woodley-vs-burns|title=Dana White announces full UFC on ESPN 9 lineup for May 30 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-05-22|access-date=2020-05-23}}\n\nAdditionally, the event included fighters that were pulled from other events previously cancelled, as well as the following bouts:\n\n*A heavyweight bout between former [[List of WSOF champions#Heavyweight Championship|WSOF Heavyweight Champion]] [[Blagoy Ivanov]] and [[Augusto Sakai]] (scheduled for the original May 9 date of [[UFC 250]] that later became [[UFC 249]]).\n*A women's strawweight bout between [[Mackenzie Dern]] and [[Hannah Cifers]] (scheduled for [[Canceled UFC event on April 25, 2020|UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Teixeira on April 25]]).\n\nOn May 26, [[Kevin Holland]] was forced to withdraw from his scheduled welterweight bout with [[Daniel Rodriguez (fighter)|Daniel Rodriguez]] due to injury.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/kevin-holland-injured-out-of-ufc-on-espn-9-bout-vs-daniel-rodriguez |title= Kevin Holland injured, out of UFC on ESPN 9 bout vs. Daniel Rodriguez|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-05-26|access-date=2020-05-26}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Gabriel Green (fighter)|Gabriel Green]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/gabriel-green-pegged-to-replace-kevin-holland-at-ufc-on-espn-9-meets-daniel-rodriguez|title=Gabriel Green expected to replace Kevin pounds, one and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit of 156 pounds. Vince Morales: $20,000\n\n^ ''Weaver was fined 20% ($2,400) of his purse for missing weight, which went to Roberts.''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn August 5, it was announced that the NSAC issued temporary suspensions for [[Jamahal Hill]] and [[Tim Elliott]], after they tested positive for [[marijuana]] in their respective pre-fight screenings.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/luis-pena-tim-elliot-jamahal-hill-get-temporary-suspensions-extended-by-nac-after-positive-tests-for-marijuana|title=Three fighters get temporary NAC suspensions extended after positive marijuana tests|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-08-05|access-date=2020-08-05}} On September 3, the NSAC announced that Hill's victory was overturned to a no contest due to the violation. He was suspended six months and fined 15% of his fight purse. Meanwhile, Elliott was suspended four and a half months and fined 15% of his fight purse. arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 13, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/6/2/21278251/jessica-eye-vs-cynthia-calvillo-main-event-official-ufc-event-june-13-mma|title=Jessica Eye vs Gerbasi|date=2020-06-14|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was initially planned to take place at [[Astana Arena]] in [[Nur-Sultan]], Kazakhstan and would have been the first that the promotion had contested there.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/25/21153512/ufc-targets-kazakhstan-for-june-13-event|title=UFC targets Kazakhstan for June 13 event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-02-25|access-date=2020-03-03}} However, it was announced by UFC president [[Dana White]] on April 9 that starting with [[UFC 249]], all future events were indefinitely postponed due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises ‘Fight Island’ will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}}\n\nWhile not officially announced by the organization, the promotion was targeting a bantamweight bout between former [[WSOF Bantamweight Championship|WSOF Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Marlon Moraes]] and [[Petr Yan]] to serve as the original event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-kazakhstan-marlon-moraes-vs-petr-yan-main-event-fight-booking|title=Marlon Moraes, Petr Yan agree to headline UFC Kazakhstan|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-03-04|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-03-04}} The pairing was scrapped after it was confirmed that the event would no longer take place in Kazakhstan.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-announces-sad-news-postpones-2-events-ufc-news/|title=UFC announces sad news; postpones two events|author=Deepit Sharma|publisher=essentiallysports.com|date=2020-05-05|access-date=2020-06-19}}\n\nSubsequently, a women's flyweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Jessica Eye]] and [[Cynthia Calvillo]] served as the new event headliner for Las Vegas. The event streamed on [[ESPN+]] and aired on [[ESPN]].\n\nDue to the event being relocated to the United States, some changes were made as several fighters were unable to compete due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]]:\n*A light heavyweight bout between [[Roman Dolidze]] and [[Khadis Ibragimov]] was postponed and rescheduled for July 18 at [[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-khadis-ibragimov-treft-roman-dolidze-tijdens-ufc-nur-sultan-in-kazachstan/|title=Khadis Ibragimov meets Roman Dolidze during UFC Nur-Sultan in Kazakhstan|author=Staff|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-03-03|access-date=2020-06-19}} {{in lang|nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-targeting-roman-dolidze-vs-khadis-ibragimov-for-july-18-event|title=UFC targeting Roman Dolidze vs. Khadis Ibragimov for July 18 event|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-11|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-06-19}}\n*A flyweight bout between promotional newcomer [[Zhalgas Zhumagulov]] and [[Jordan Espinosa]] was initially scheduled for the event when it was targeted for Kazakhstan.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/zhalgas-zhumagulov-vs-jordan-espinosa-ufc-kazakhstan|title=UFC Kazakhstan adds Zhalgas Zhumagulov homecoming vs. Jordan Espinosa|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-03|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-06-19}} However, due to travel restrictions related to COVID-19 pandemic, Zhumagulov was removed from the card after the event was moved to Las Vegas. Espinosa instead would face [[Mark De La Rosa]] in a bantamweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-adds-jordan-espinosa-vs-mark-de-la-rosa-to-june-13-event|title=UFC adds Jordan Espinosa vs. Mark De La Rosa to June 13 event|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-05-20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-06-19}}\n\nAdditionally, the event included fighters that were pulled from other events previously cancelled, as well as the following bouts:{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/4/21281107/full-fight-card-set-for-ufc-fight-night-event-on-june-13-calvillo-vs-eye-headlines|title=Full fight card set for UFC Fight Night event on June 13, Calvillo vs. Eye headlines|author=Newswire|date=2020-06-04|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2020-06-06}}\n\n*A middleweight bout between [[Karl Roberson]] and [[Marvin Vettori]] (originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Teixeira]], but Roberson pulled out due to issues related to his failed weight cut).{{cite web|url=https://www.yahoo.com/sports/ufc-smith-vs-teixeira-main-153325277.html|title=UFC: Smith vs. Teixeira main card bout canceled due to medical issues|publisher=yahoo.com|author=Ken Pishna|date=2020-05-13|access-date=2020-05-13}}\n*A women's bantamweight bout between [[Julia Avila]] and [[Karol Rosa]]. The pairing was expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Maia vs. Askren]] in October 2019, but Rosa pulled out due to a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/es/noticia/karol-rosa-lesiona-o-joelho-e-esta-fora-da-luta-com-julia-avila-no-ufc-singapura.ghtml|title=Karol Rosa injures her knee and is out of the fight with Julia Avila at UFC Singapore|author=Staff|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|date=2019-10-16|access-date=2020-06-06}} {{in lang|pt}} They were later rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Hermansson vs. Weidman]], but both times the bout was postponed due to the COVID-19 pandemic.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-postpones-three-events-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC postpones three events amid growing coronavirus outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-06-06}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228718/ufc-officially-cancels-four-events-due-to-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC officially cancels four events due to coronavirus pandemic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-06-06}} They were later booked for this event, but the bout was canceled for the fourth time as Rosa had [[Travel visa|visa]] issues. She was replaced by [[Gina Mazany]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/4/21281036/with-karol-rosa-out-julia-avila-now-faces-gina-mazany-at-ufc-event-on-june-13|title=With Karol Rosa out, Julia Avila now faces Gina Mazany at UFC event on June 13|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-06-04|access-date=2020-06-06}}\n*A women's flyweight bout between former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's flyweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Ariane Lipski]] and [[Luana Carolina]] (originally scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Overeem vs. Harris]]). Subsequently, the pairing was delayed a second time and was rescheduled for July 18 at [[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]] due to travel restrictions for both fighters.{{cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/update-ariane-lipski-vs-luana-carolina-rescheduled-for-ufc-fight-island-event-on-july-18th/|title=Ariane Lipski vs. Luana Carolina rescheduled for UFC Fight Island event on July 18th|publisher=fightbookmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2020-06-15|access-date=2020-06-15}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Melissa Gatto]] and [[Mariya Agapova]] was scheduled for the event, but Gatto pulled out on June 7 due to visa issues.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Melissa-Gatto-Withdraws-from-Saturdays-UFC-Fight-Night-Card-Due-to-Visa-Issues-173313|title=Melissa Gatto withdraws from Saturday’s UFC Fight Night card due to Visa issues|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-06-07|access-date=2020-06-08}} She was replaced by [[Hannah Cifers]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-hannah-cifers-quick-turnaround-replacement-vs-mariya-agapova|title=Hannah Cifers makes two-week turnaround to fight Mariya Agapova on Saturday|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-08|access-date=2020-06-08}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Tyson Nam]] and [[Ryan Benoit]] was scheduled for the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/05/ufc-june-13-ryan-benoit-tyson-nam-flyweight-bout|title=UFC adds Ryan Benoit vs. Tyson Nam to June 13 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-05-24|access-date=2021-01-20}} However, Benoit withdrew due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Zarrukh Adashev]], thus changing the contest to the bantamweight division.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-hannah-cifers-quick-turnaround-replacement-vs-mariya-agapova|title=With Ryan Benoit out of UFC on ESPN 10, Zarrukh Adashev steps in to fight Tyson Nam|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-11}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ray Borg]] and [[Merab Dvalishvili]] was scheduled for the event. However, Borg withdrew from the event for personal reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Gustavo Lopez (fighter)|Gustavo Lopez]]. The bout took place at a catchweight of 140 pounds.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ray-borg-out-of-ufc-on-espn-10-merab-dvalisvhili-now-fights-combate-americas-champ-gustavo-lopez|title=Ray Borg out of UFC on ESPN 10, Dvalisvhili now fights Combate Americas champ Lopez (UPDATE)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-06-11|access-date=2020-06-11}}\n\nBefore the weigh-ins, [[Darrick Minner]] was pulled from his bout with [[Jordan Griffin]] due to unspecified health issues and the bout was cancelled.{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/statement-darrick-minner|title=Statement on Darrick Minner|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-06-12|access-date=2020-06-12}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Eye, Roberson and Adashev all missed weight for their respective bouts. Eye weighed in at 126.25 pounds, a quarter pound over the flyweight non-title fight limit. Roberson weighed in at 190.5 pounds, four and a half pounds over the middleweight non-title fight limit and Adashev weighed in at 138.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. Their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were respectively fined 25%, 30% and 20% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents Calvillo, Vettori and Nam.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.au/mma/story/_/id/29303122/jessica-eye-expected-fight-cynthia-calvillo-missing-weigh|title=Jessica Eye one of three fighters to miss weight ahead of UFC Fight fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/14/21290580/ufc-on-espn-10-bonuses-marvin-vettori-leads-pack-of-first-time-winners|title=UFC on ESPN 10 bonuses: Lee|date=2020-06-14|access-date=2020-06-14}}\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' No bonus awarded.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Marvin Vettori, Mariya Agapova, Tyson Nam''' and '''Christian Aguilera'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC). It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $782,000.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-on-espn-10-salaries-cynthia-calvillo-andre-fili-top-782k-payroll|title=UFC on ESPN 10 salaries: Cynthia Calvillo, Andre Fili top $782k payroll|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2020-06-15|access-date=2020-06-16}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/15/21292115/cynthia-calvillo-andre-fili-lead-ufc-on-espn-10-salaries-with-110k-each|title=Cynthia Calvillo, Andre Fili lead UFC on ESPN 10 salaries with $110k each|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-06-15|access-date=2020-06-16}}\n* '''Cynthia Calvillo:''' $122,750 (includes $55,000 win bonus) def. Jessica Eye: $38,250 ^\n* '''Marvin Vettori:''' $98,000 (includes $43,000 win bonus) def. Karl Roberson: $28,000 ^\n* '''Charles Rosa:''' $68,000 (includes $34,000 win bonus) def. Kevin Aguilar: $25,000\n* '''Andre Fili:''' $110,000 (includes $55,000 win bonus) def. Charles Jourdain: $16,000\n* '''Jordan Espinosa:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Mark De La Rosa: $21,000\n* '''Mariya Agapova:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Hannah Cifers: $35,000\n* '''Merab Dvalishvili:''' $46,000 (includes $23,000 win bonus) def. Gustavo Lopez: $12,000\n* '''Julia Avila:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Gina Mazany: $16,000\n* '''Tyson Nam:''' $30,800 (includes $14,000 win bonus) def. Zarrukh Adashev: $11,200 ^\n* '''Christian Aguilera:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def.
